#### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P7: The title and Chapter1 cards lead to Sui, Sumire and Shizuka in the rehearsal-room background. Sumire asks why Sui wanted to speak; Shizuka guesses a proposal about SiSH's future activity. Sumire offers to listen before knowing its content. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0137.json__29303b34340e0c2b?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0137.json__29303b34340e0c2b?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`.

The earlier request and collective response are Sui's retrospective account here, not newly staged scenes. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0137.json__29303b34340e0c2b?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0137.json__29303b34340e0c2b?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`.

Sumire's hesitant response leaves that generalization unconfirmed. Sui has chosen her two nearby friends as models and renews her request. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0137.json__29303b34340e0c2b?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0137.json__29303b34340e0c2b?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0245-001 — Sui initiates an experiment in self-presentation: the grammatical center is the girl she herself wants to become, rather than a demand from her peers that she abandon her current manner**

Sui initiates an experiment in self-presentation: the grammatical center is the girl she herself wants to become, rather than a demand from her peers that she abandon her current manner. Her continued ボク and preference for action films matter because the new aspiration has not erased those aspects of her speech or taste. Neither Shizuka's question nor the comedy establishes a male past, a diagnosis, a settled gender history, or permanent feminization.

The chapter separates moral willingness from technical authority. Shizuka answers serious consultation with seriousness before possessing a usable definition. ### Chapter2 — 503013702

#### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P5: At the shopping-street setting, Sumire proposes beginning with appearance, acknowledges the simplicity of that approach, and leads toward a familiar shop. Sui notices girls in cute clothing. Sumire names long skirts and one-piece dresses as conventional lady choices, while explicitly wanting to preserve what is characteristic of Sui. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0138.json__b419815569c6334e?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0138.json__b419815569c6334e?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`.
- P6–P12: Sui is drawn to sneakers for their advertised cushioning and reduced damage to the feet, and wonders about comfort. Sumire says she thinks ladies do not wear sneakers much; Sui accepts the correction. Shizuka makes a sneaker/skip pun and laughs in the written dialogue. Sumire notices her enjoyment. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0138.json__b419815569c6334e?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0138.json__b419815569c6334e?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`.
- P13–P20: Sumire proposes a semi-long skirt meant to combine elegance with Sui's liveliness. Sui evaluates whether it will impede running and says she dislikes long skirts clinging around her legs. Sumire responds that she thinks ladies do not run around indiscriminately; Sui again accepts the premise. **CL-B0245-006 — Sumire's aim is accommodation rather than the simple elimination of Sui's existing style: her initial criterion is to retain Sui-like qualities, and the proposed semi-long skirt is her attempt to combine elegance with activity**

Sumire's aim is accommodation rather than the simple elimination of Sui's existing style: her initial criterion is to retain Sui-like qualities, and the proposed semi-long skirt is her attempt to combine elegance with activity. Dictionary lookup resolves Shizuka's lexical uncertainty without eliminating the problem of teaching. ### Chapter4 — 503013704

#### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P4: Back in the rehearsal-room setting, Sui and Sumire acknowledge the difficulty of becoming a lady. Shizuka apologizes for not helping more. Sui refuses the need for apology and says their cooperation itself made her happy. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4`.
- P5–P17: Sumire asks why Sui chose her, distinguishing herself from Shizuka's recognizable young-lady status. Sui credits Sumire with fashion knowledge, attention to her surroundings and an older-sister quality. Shizuka interprets lady as Sui's projected ideal and admiration, not merely demureness; Sui explicitly confirms that both friends are wonderful ladies to her. Both recipients resist treating themselves as wholly exemplary yet acknowledge pleasure in being admired. Sumire thanks Sui; Sui is surprised her statement merits thanks. They still have no next idea. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`.
- P18–P27: Player welcomes the returned friends, then falls. Sui asks whether he is all right; Shizuka says his papers have scattered and offers to help; Sumire notices their content. Player says these include proposals for work he declined and intended to shred. Sumire reads a pitch about bringing out a new side of SiSH and business only a lady can do. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`.
- P28–P40: Sui seizes on lady. Player reports that the company sent costumes in advance along with its unusually enthusiastic proposal, and that he thought the members would probably dislike it. Sui asks if doing it would make her a lady; no affirmative answer is supplied. Sumire asks to hear the proposal before destruction, and Shizuka asks for a chance to try. Player proposes judging after trying the delivered costumes. Sui agrees, Sumire moves toward starting, and Shizuka asks where the costumes are. Player says they are in the costume room and offers to guide them, but closes by asking whether they are really sure. No job or costume trial is completed in this chapter. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0245-016 — Sui supplies a result the failed practical lessons did not measure: she valued the friends' willingness to cooperate**

Sui supplies a result the failed practical lessons did not measure: she valued the friends' willingness to cooperate. Her explanation of why Sumire was chosen also gives the apparently ordinary helper a different view of herself. Sumire's fashion knowledge and attention to others, rather than inherited status alone, enter Sui's lady ideal. Shizuka's interpretation is then explicitly endorsed by Sui, making it more than an analyst's projection.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0140.json__98c36470316504e9?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`.

**CL-B0245-017 — The exchange keeps admired people from becoming flawless standards**

The exchange keeps admired people from becoming flawless standards. Sumire and Shizuka each hesitate over the idealization while accepting the pleasure of being valued. Sui does not initially recognize the effect of her own praise. Knowledge moves both ways between pupil and model: the helpers learn what they mean to her, and their thanks reveals an interpersonal consequence she had not anticipated.

An accidental spill reopens a managerial decision the members had not considered in the shown exchange. The local episode therefore exposes a gap between presumed protection and available member choice without proving a universal institutional policy.

- P9–P19: The costume-room background and three new costume sprite families appear. Sumire objects to Player entering. He invokes the statement that changing was finished. Sui and Shizuka ask how they look; he says the clothes suit them while explicitly withholding a judgment about being a lady. Sumire thanks him, then asks why the outfits are bunny costumes. Player says the costume and project concept do not match and repeats his reason for intending to refuse. Sumire calls the attempt a mistake. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`.

Sumire and Shizuka respond with surprise or questioning rather than validating it. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`.

Sui requests help from the two friends, and Shizuka says she will ask Mana about it. The chapter ends with Sumire's exasperation; no contact, inquiry to Mana, bodily change, job acceptance or performance is enacted. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0141.json__398b818dcaac78bf?doc=primary&log=40#/Pages/40`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0245-021 — The costume trial produces differentiated reactions rather than a shared verdict**

The costume trial produces differentiated reactions rather than a shared verdict. Sumire objects to the outfit and to entry, while Sui and Shizuka solicit an appraisal. Player's response explicitly separates suitability of clothing from successful attainment of 'lady'. The commercial pitch therefore does not deliver the stable definition or guaranteed transformation that Sui sought.

### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- Chapter1 establishes a personal consultation among the three SiSH members. Sui wants help becoming the girl she wants to be and calls her two admired friends ladies. Sumire and Shizuka agree to help before possessing an agreed definition. Their planned outing contrasts with Player's subsequent arrival for a work consultation.
- Chapter2 attempts appearance first. Sumire explicitly tries to preserve Sui-like liveliness, but her corrections about sneakers and running narrow that aspiration. Sui values cushioning, comfort and free movement; Shizuka offers a dress like her reported home clothing and enjoys two puns. Goods are displayed; no purchase or trying-on of those shopping items is established.
- Chapter3 moves from a dictionary synonym and Shizuka's long-acquired comportment to a teaching exercise. Sui's own swimming knowledge supplies observation, imitation and repeated practice. Sumire stages greetings; Shizuka praises an initially Sui-like response while Sumire asks for adjustment. Copying the model's puns exposes the selectivity implicit in the instruction to imitate.
- Chapter4 returns to the rehearsal-room setting. Sui says cooperation itself made her happy and explains her particular admiration for Sumire's fashion knowledge and attentiveness. Both friends are pleased but resist idealization. Player's fall scatters previously declined work proposals; the members discover a lady-labelled SiSH pitch and ask to hear more. He reports expecting their dislike and proposes evaluating delivered costumes. No full job acceptance is established.
- Chapter5 stages an outfit trial, an entry objection, qualified compliments and the explicit bunny-costume/concept mismatch. Shizuka links her own ideal to Mana, while Sui draws an unverified bodily rule. A nested report about Mana and Nicole prompts further misunderstanding and Sumire's correction. The end card closes the Event with an intended inquiry to Mana and continuing objections, without a settled lady definition, job performance or bodily result.

#### Causal structure

- **Link:** Personal aspiration → named peer consultation

**Effect:** Sui selects people she admires, not an externally mandated correction of her identity.

- **Link:** Whole-person imitation → copied puns → objection to selection

**Effect:** Sui exposes the difference between copying a person and selecting only the features the coach meant.
- **Link:** Acknowledged unsuccessful instruction → gratitude and particular admiration

**Basis:** C4P1–16

- **Link:** Fall/spilled papers → disclosed proposal → member interest

**Basis:** C4P18–39

- **Link:** Costume reveal → conflicting reception → new model/unsupported inference

**Basis:** C5P9–40

**Effect:** The pitch does not resolve lady status. Different appraisals and a nested report produce renewed questions and resistance instead of final mastery.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Sui's earlier request to Player and being corrected by everyone are retrospective reports in Chapter1. Shizuka's male-past question is a misunderstanding, not biographical proof. No gender-history diagnosis follows.
- Shizuka's dictionary lookup is supplied only in Chapter3; it must not be backdated into her Chapter1 uncertainty. Her long training is a self-report that explains tacit familiarity, not a comprehensive externally graded competence.
- Sumire's appearance and behavior rules are attributed, often tentative 'I think' judgments. Sui's acceptance of them does not make them universal facts about women. Different helpers' appraisals remain distinct.
- Sui's admiration is explicit before the helpers fully understand why they were chosen. Their appreciation does not turn them into flawless exemplars; Sui's surprise at being thanked preserves uneven understanding.
- Player's Chapter1 absence interpretation is not a verified home destination. Chapter4's return exchange supplies a later local return relation, not the missing itinerary or exact duration.
- The company pitch and Player's expectations are reported content. The shown discovery reopens a refusal but does not supply all terms, a prior consultation, a completed engagement or a universal institutional rule.
- Sui's statement that changing is finished is not Sumire's permission to enter. Sumire's thanks for a compliment does not cancel her entry objection, costume objection or objection to redirected questioning.
- Shizuka reports what Mana said about Nicole. The absent participants, circumstances, consent and claimed frequency are not independently witnessed. Sumire's subsequent Coney remark is a local association, not a new all-cast identity-disclosure scene. Canonical spelling Coney; stable local token unchanged.
- Sui's body rule, milk question and massage analogy are not established knowledge or recommendations. Sumire explicitly rejects the post-swimming equivalence. No touching, effect, consultation with Mana or final assent occurs before the end card.
- Catalog order binds analytical consumption only. Local reports, return and changed clothes establish particular before/after relations; no late-Main calendar, current all-participant knowledge horizon, specific weekend-event identity or next-family causal connection is inferred.

#### Character relations

- **Subject:** Sui

**Finding:** Her effort is self-initiated and relational: admiration makes Sumire and Shizuka useful models, and their willingness to help has value before a successful method exists. Athletic body-use knowledge shapes both shopping and learning. A useful procedural swimming analogy and a later explicitly rejected analogy must remain distinct. Her continued ボク, preferences and agency coexist with trying unfamiliar forms; no permanent transformation or settled self-rejection is established.
- **Subject:** Sumire

**Finding:** Practical fashion mediation, explicit doubts, corrective coaching and embarrassment at being admired coexist. Her attempted accommodation is not free of restrictive assumptions, and she is entitled to distinct objections during the costume sequence. Repeated exasperation does not cancel care; care does not erase her objections. Sui's praise offers a particular counterview to Sumire's reluctance to see herself as a lady.
- **Subject:** Shizuka

**Finding:** A pledged response to a serious consultation precedes lexical understanding. She later supplies dictionary work, reports a family-trained manner and participates in teaching without possessing an easy pedagogy. Her puns belong to the actual person Sui is copying. Being Sui's model does not prevent her from having her own model in Mana, experiencing uncertainty or seeking further information.
- **Subject:** Player

**Finding:** A work consultation initially misses the outing; later an accidental spill reveals a proposal he had anticipated the members would dislike. He offers a limited outfit-trial decision route, qualifies his compliment and remains vulnerable to a privacy objection. This local pattern supports examining withheld information and differentiated member choice, not a categorical competence or governance verdict.
- **Subject:** Mana and Nicole/Coney

**Finding:** Mana functions as Shizuka's admired model and as a differently framed public image for Sui. Nicole appears through a nested retrospective report, followed by Sumire's Coney allusion. No new direct conduct or interior state of either absent person is supplied.
- **Subject:** SiSH

**Finding:** The same three members pursue a personal inquiry through complementary but imperfect assistance, explicit admiration, practical disagreement and different reception of a costume pitch. Their earlier older-sister unit abstraction remains relational rather than an exhaustive personality rule; no unit maturity or technical-performance promotion follows.

#### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P14: At the gathering, Coney checks attendance and Player reports that the late Momoka has arrived. Haru anticipates a group camp, Musubi says the announcement was sudden, Momoka would rather have stayed home, Kajika expects fun, and Sui anticipates both lessons and play. Hime questions uniforms; Musubi invokes school rules, which Haru qualifies as probably specific to St Corsica. Sumire and Rona ask about the still-unannounced destination. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`.
- P15–P34: Coney announces Shizuka/Kuonji financial backing. Shizuka says she arranged accommodation and reports her grandfather willingly offered cooperation. Player checks whether that is all right. Coney then asks where the camp is, surprising Player, and attributes not knowing to busyness. Shizuka names a family-owned uninhabited island and a family jet. Her request to remain still on the jet initially prompts Musubi's safety interpretation, then resolves into a pun anticipated by Sumire and Sui. Coney calls this Nanasta's first summer camp and launches the outing. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34`.
- P35–P47: An inserted title card separates the next setting. The group now reacts to the Kuonji villa: Sui, Musubi and Rona express astonishment, Susu praises furnishings, Haru thinks of cleaning, Hime questions that reaction, Momoka asks for a drink, Kajika is excited, and Shinju reminds her to behave in someone else's house. Sawara imagines a lion-shaped bath spout. Shizuka modestly welcomes them. The bath image is Sawara's expectation, not a shown fixture. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47`.
- P48–P56: Coney announces room allocation. Shizuka asks whether individual rooms are truly unnecessary; Coney favors shared rooms for fun, closeness and possible nighttime talk. Shizuka makes a further pun. Momoka wants to lie down and Sumire supports ending the standing conversation. A black transition follows; exact room partners have not been supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=48#/Pages/48`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56`.
- P57–P69: Coney acknowledges Player has put down his luggage. He feels bad about alone having a private room. Coney turns this into two joking romantic/sexual insinuations, first about the idols and then herself, while invoking their business-like relationship; Player rejects both interpretations. She abruptly returns to work: check whether everyone has settled, then announce practice if appropriate. He accepts that explanation. The chapter ends before the room checks or lessons are shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=57#/Pages/57`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0142.json__45e73ed10708c908?doc=primary&log=69#/Pages/69`.

#### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P9: Kajika and Haru's opening words already respond to a comparison. Player enters to ask about unpacking; Momoka asks whether he sees the resemblance, and Haru explains that Momoka thought Haru and Kajika look like sisters together. Player says he had not especially noticed, usually seeing Kajika with Sawara and Shinju. Coney suggests that one could even call them a sister unit. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`.
- P10–P17: Haru playfully introduces herself with the Harumi surname; Kajika uses Kasukabe, so Player points out that swapping in opposite directions does not give them a common sister surname. They acknowledge the oversight. Momoka and Coney then use their shared absent-mindedness as another playful basis for the comparison. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`.
- P18–P23: Haru says she wanted a younger sister like Kajika. Kajika reciprocates the wish for an older sister nearer her own age while explicitly affirming her love for Sawara. Coney reframes this as a Harumi Sisters withdrawal announcement; Kajika denies it. She imagines Sawara chasing her if she tried to run away, quoting an imagined pursuit. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`.
- P24–P28: Player finds the anecdote not quite as heartwarming as it sounds. Haru says she too would chase her younger brother if he ran. Kajika generalizes that this is what older sisters are like; Player qualifies that not all older sisters want to chase. The chapter fades without a literal escape, pursuit, legal kinship change or formal new unit. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0245-040 — The room scene makes resemblance an occasion for mutually desired affinity rather than biological evidence**

The room scene makes resemblance an occasion for mutually desired affinity rather than biological evidence. Haru and Kajika each state what the imagined relation would offer. Their name-switching joke makes the distinction legible: a playful introduction is neither a changed identity nor a registration of kinship, and even the improvised performance fails the simple same-name premise.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0143.json__986f4b2077e05354?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`.

Closeness is additive in Kajika's own formulation. She wishes for a near-age older-sister figure while explicitly preserving love for Sawara. #### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P12: Player finds Sawara with a camera coaching Sui's posing while Susu participates. Sui calls this gravure-photo practice. Susu judges her movement stiff; Sawara recalls that, asked for a confident pose, Sui chose a pre-dive swimming pose. Sui reports that club girls had called it cool; Coney responds with her prince label. Sawara presents herself as helping. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0144.json__ab76258a2996dc4b?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0144.json__ab76258a2996dc4b?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`.
- P13–P19: Player questions whether the activity is all right. Sawara assures him there will be no accidental exposure; he objects to her bluntness. Coney suggests an accident would be unavoidable, and Player explicitly rejects that. Sawara asks him to watch another attempt. Sui agrees, explicitly saying she is trying to become a lady. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0144.json__ab76258a2996dc4b?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0144.json__ab76258a2996dc4b?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`.
- P20–P30: Asked for a leopard-like pose and given Susu's explanation, Sui associates the animal with speed and takes what Player identifies as a crouching start. Sawara praises its dynamism and invites her to run. Sui starts; Susu then says she really has run away from the scene. Sawara praises the sports-girl image and announces she will follow to photograph her. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0144.json__ab76258a2996dc4b?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0144.json__ab76258a2996dc4b?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`.

#### Factual reconstruction

- P0–P12: Shizuka is already showing a turn to Musubi and Hime in the lounge. Hime asks for repetition and judges her own attempt too slow. Coney and Player arrive after finding their room empty. Shizuka says changing finished early, so they began lesson preparation. Hime finds the model difficult; Musubi praises its sharpness and voices anxiety. Shizuka encourages shared practice. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`.
- P13–P22: Shizuka makes a practice-related pun. Musubi and Hime praise her in different terms; Coney reports SiSH dance reception. Shizuka credits repeated practice with Sui. Musubi resolves not to fall behind; Hime asks for tips. Shizuka agrees to pass on what Sui taught while qualifying her own ability to explain. The managers describe the transmission from Sui through Shizuka to others as a good camp-like flow. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`.
- P23–P36: Shizuka explains a turn and movement through compressed sound-symbolic instructions, attributing them to Sui. Hime cannot interpret them; Musubi attempts an explicit waist/rotation paraphrase, but Shizuka cannot confirm it and repeats the original shorthand. Her further demonstration earns Hime's recognition that she can do it, while Musubi still does not understand its meaning. The chapter distinguishes demonstrated ability, explanatory ability and uptake. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`.
- P37–P47: After a transition, Player says the round of checks is complete. Coney is satisfied that everyone seems to be enjoying camp; he objects that scheduled lessons still lie ahead. She foregrounds the sea after lessons and teases him about anticipating swimwear. His hesitant response is interpreted by Coney as desire, without a completed confession. She proposes finishing lessons quickly, and Player ends by questioning whether this camp will be all right. No scheduled lesson result or beach arrival is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0146.json__3a6691de024e6a64?doc=primary&log=47#/Pages/47`.

The rehearsal realizes part of the camp's learning aim before the managers formally start it: peers have independently gathered to practice, Hime requests another demonstration, and Musubi expresses a particular gap between the admired model and her own confidence. ### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- Chapter1 gathers the Nanasta ensemble for a suddenly announced camp. The destination becomes known only after questions: Shizuka reports family support, an island and private jet. Arrival at the villa prompts differentiated reactions. Coney chooses shared rooms rather than Shizuka's offered individual accommodation and later announces checks before scheduled practice. Player rejects her romantic insinuations about his private room.
- Chapter2 visits Haru, Kajika and Momoka. Momoka's sister comparison prompts playful exchanged surnames and reciprocal wishes for a sister-like relation. Kajika explicitly preserves affection for Sawara and denies Coney's mock withdrawal. Hypothetical sibling pursuit is qualified by Player; no new kinship or unit is formed.
- Chapter3 finds Sawara, Sui and Susu in photo-practice talk. Sui's athletic vocabulary redirects the requested presentation toward running, which Sawara values photographically. Player rejects Coney's inevitability-of-accident rationale. Susu reports Sui has run off; after Sawara announces pursuit, Player says both have gone. Camera interest becomes new information for him; Susu's requested photographer remains a wish.
- Chapter4 visits Rona, Sumire and Shinju during a conversation about swimwear. Peer appraisals produce embarrassment and objections. Sumire questions Player's gaze; he repeatedly denies it and rejects Coney answering a preference for him. Neither a swimsuit display nor a settled motive or physical touching is supplied.
- Chapter5 finds Shizuka, Musubi and Hime preparing for lessons. Shizuka credits Sui and demonstrates according to the peers' appraisals, but her transmitted shorthand does not become intelligible instruction for the recipients. The managers finish their checks, with scheduled lessons and the sea still ahead. Player's final concern leaves the camp's outcome open.

- **Link:** Sudden collective plan → destination questions → Shizuka's disclosed resources

**Finding:** A failed intended pose becomes an opportunity to value Sui's actual movement. No final photograph or lady attainment follows.
- **Link:** Private topic plus manager arrival → changed reception and accusation

- **Link:** Sui practice → Shizuka attribution → peer teaching attempt → failed uptake

**Finding:** Knowledge circulation is explicitly named, then tested: embodying a move and reproducing its words do not guarantee that new recipients understand it.

**Finding:** The scheduled lesson and sea are announced next steps rather than results already achieved by this family's endpoint.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Attendance is represented by named dialogue and a literal group reaction. 'Everyone' does not establish all49 Nanasta portfolios or every member's consent. All13 named portrait subjects plus Player are covered by admitted prior states.
- Shizuka reports her grandfather's willingness, family island and jet; Coney supplies the broad backing claim. These do not erase previous family constraints, establish a full budget, staffing or transport procedure, or prove unlimited future permission.
- Musubi's uniform claim is explicitly narrowed by Haru to a probable school-specific rule. Sawara's lion-bath expectation remains an imagined feature, not a photographed facility.
- Coney's room-sharing rationale, romantic insinuations and readings of Player are her framings. Player denies the first two and later protests premature satisfaction; his uncompleted hesitation is not a certified desire or preference.
- Haru/Kajika name play, sister affinity and Coney's possible unit branding do not change names, kinship, household membership or lineup. Kajika denies withdrawal; hypothetical pursuit remains conditional, and Player rejects its universality.
- Sawara's assurance of no accidental exposure is not a verified safeguard. Coney's claim that an accident would be unavoidable is explicitly rejected. No incident, injury, public-photo release or comprehensive consent policy is enacted.
- C3 has explicit departure reports by Susu and Player after Sawara's announced follow-up; those text accounts are stronger than an intention alone, while no runtime exit or resulting photograph was observed. A fading retained portrait cannot override the spoken account.
- Rona's stated embarrassment, Sumire's objections, Shinju's asserted factualness and Player's denials are distinct. A startled exclamation does not establish physical touching; an accusation does not independently establish motive.
- Shizuka's attribution to Sui and the peers' praise support a local teaching history and perceived execution. The final inability to understand the shorthand remains. Neither technical performance nor competence across every teaching context is independently graded.
- The room checks have local serial continuity through repeated entry/check language and Player's closing statement. A common room background does not supply exact accommodation assignment. No later lesson or beach outcome is backdated into this frozen endpoint.
- Source title 前編 identifies this as the front part. 後編 remains unopened at this freeze: no final outcome or causal relation beyond source-explicit prospective plans is imported from it. The label alone is not a global calendar.

#### Character relations

- **Subject:** Shizuka

**Finding:** Hosts through a particular family contribution, modestly checks accommodation preferences, enjoys puns and shares practice. Her ability to model and her difficulty translating instruction coexist. Reported generosity does not erase permission history or make her an all-purpose expert.
- **Subject:** Coney and Player

**Finding:** Their managerial partnership alternates organization, teasing, objection and work reminders. Coney names closeness and knowledge circulation as benefits; Player repeatedly protects the limits of what he has said and objects to an accident rationale. These local actions neither certify mature governance nor reduce either person to universal incompetence or omniscience.
- **Subject:** Haru and Kajika

**Finding:** Reciprocal desired sister-like affinity expands interpersonal belonging without replacing Kajika's explicitly valued family. Momoka's comic observation and Coney's branding tease receive different kinds of uptake; a possible concept is not institutional formation.
- **Subject:** Sui and Sawara

**Finding:** An athletic self-understanding persists through a lady-labelled photographic exercise. Sawara's interest shifts toward the sports image rather than proving the originally requested presentation succeeded. Her camera enthusiasm becomes reported new information for Player, not a measured photographic skill grade.
- **Subject:** Susu

**Finding:** Offers a critical appraisal, explains a cue, reports Sui's departure and expresses her own photographer preference. Those acts do not supply objective performance knowledge, a completed shoot, reciprocal desire or an all-purpose consent position.
- **Subject:** Rona, Sumire and Shinju

**Finding:** Praise, reassurance, blunt comparison, embarrassment and objection circulate asymmetrically. Rona's giving a compliment does not imply comfort receiving every appraisal; Sumire's challenge does not settle Player's interiority; Shinju's certainty does not make her norm universal or recipient-neutral.
- **Subject:** Musubi and Hime

**Finding:** Both actively seek practice rather than merely receive instruction. Musubi attempts a concrete paraphrase and voices anxiety; Hime asks repetition and distinguishes recognizing an executable demonstration from understanding it. No mastery result is shown.
- **Subject:** Nanasta and Harumi sisters

**Finding:** Nanasta's camp provides shared opportunities and friction without a global institutional reform. UNIT-0006's family/shop care and labor distinctions remain; Kajika's explicit denial prevents a false unit-departure update. The offered peer affinity does not establish biological kinship.

#### Literary synthesis with retained alternatives

**CL-B0245-062 — Collective logistics do not produce equal knowledge or appetite**

The camp is announced before everyone knows the destination, including an organizer. Different enthusiasms, reluctance and practical concerns persist. Shizuka's reported family support enables the venue without demonstrating complete costs, consent or staffing.

- P13–P24: Sawara admires Susu's reported independent arrival and life in this country. She speculates that absence of regret and loneliness can be different matters, and gives that as her reason for wanting to indulge Susu as an older sister. Sui first admires Sawara, then adds that Susu is not alone because the others are with her. Sawara accepts that addition. Susu complains that the two are noisy. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0149.json__eb00b3488ff8bb5f?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0149.json__eb00b3488ff8bb5f?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0245-080 — The first interval allows a real account of dependence alongside an asserted self-image of independence**

The first interval allows a real account of dependence alongside an asserted self-image of independence. Susu requests practical help and resists the child label; Sawara supplies an age comparison and a hypothesis about loneliness, while Sui adds present companionship. The lonely-without-regret distinction is Sawara's interpretation, not Susu's confirmed interior confession. Companionship does not logically disprove every form of loneliness.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0245-091 — The opening makes interpretation depend on delayed context**

The opening makes interpretation depend on delayed context. Kajika's protest and Momoka's aggression initially lack a visible object; the game explanation supplies it. The manager's uncertainty is textually supported, but Coney's insinuation cannot be promoted into a confession or a new interpersonal act. This scene's playful resolution cannot erase the separately identified boundary problem in the earlier Sawara/Susu room.

**CL-B0245-092 — Player's chaperone practice is concrete and selective: he asks about an ambiguous disturbance, accepts the benign game explanation, sets a noise limit, then refuses managerial initiation of a nighttime tournament**

Player's chaperone practice is concrete and selective: he asks about an ambiguous disturbance, accepts the benign game explanation, sets a noise limit, then refuses managerial initiation of a nighttime tournament. Coney treats management as participation and entertainment, while Player names the supervisory obligation. Their joking coexistence does not establish an institution-wide safeguarding system, completed intervention in the earlier room, or universal agreement with the wake-up prank.

#### Original questions and limits (historical at this exit)

- The proposed surprise live's execution, repertoire, audience response, technical quality and group practice outcome.
- The exact content and means of the reported wake-up prank, injury status, and any wider agreement or supervision policy.

- Extracted text, complete native fields and selected still resources were reviewed; no performed audio, motion or runtime playback was heard or viewed.

### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- Chapter1 explicitly resumes after dinner and recalls the beach, establishing continuity with the already frozen 前編. Members report different activities and pleasures: Momoka's game-like scuba experience, Rona's shells, Shizuka's first water-pistol play, watermelon, beach volleyball and Sawara's photographs. Reports do not expose the original activities or verify every achievement. Musubi's starfish distress prompts Haru's check, without a diagnosis. Player announces bedtime rounds and a planned return the next day; Coney's romantic-eavesdropping explanation is denied.
- Chapter2 returns to Shizuka, Musubi and Hime doing schoolwork. Musubi explains a translation exercise and Shizuka praises the explanation. Further questions lead to a tea compromise, with its completion not shown. Their reports about school subjects differentiate local curricula. Shizuka's imagined market arithmetic is corrected as an imaginative association, not made into a factual or ability deficit.
- Chapter3 shows Susu asking Sawara to carry her, Sawara agreeing, and Sui responding to their older-sister framing. Sawara explicitly calls Susu fourteen in this local scene, compares her with Kajika, and speculates about loneliness abroad. Susu resists the child label and does not confirm that inner state. A black manager cut precedes a later revelation of already ongoing intimate touching by Sawara. Sui objects; Susu supplies no explicit assent. Sui's own participation is not established, nor do the managers learn or endorse the final reveal. The request to be carried does not authorize a different act.
- Chapter4 follows Sumire's skincare offer to Shinju and Rona. Shinju initially says she can apply powder herself; Rona offers help using Coney's advice. Rona's description of Sumire's kindness gives Sumire valued recognition and accompanies Rona's moment of local confidence. Shinju corrects a proposed picture of her household: she does not usually sleep together as Rona supposes. No final sleeping arrangement or medical effect is shown.
- Chapter5 initially withholds portraits and background, then explains Kajika's protests and Momoka's theatrical aggression as a puzzle-game rivalry, with Haru encouraging both players. Player sets a noise limit and later rejects Coney's proposed manager-led pillow fight. The airport-set closing recalls a wake-up prank, keeping Player's objection and Musubi's counter-report about attempted waking. It differentiates members' plans, including occasional hair-play permission and continuing dance practice. Coney proposes a surprise Nanasta streamed live; objections precede a collective rally addressed to 777☆SISTERS. The fade and 終わり end the camp account before the live is performed.

#### Causal structure

- **Link:** Dinner and recalled sea activities → bedtime rounds

**Basis:** C1P1–47

**Finding:** Shared time creates reports and local reactions that the chaperones encounter; the source explicitly links the back part to the front part, without retrospectively displaying the lesson or beach.
- **Link:** Requested explanation → successful local uptake → more questions → compromise

**Basis:** C2P1–37

**Finding:** Musubi's intelligible translation explanation contrasts with the front part's failed dance-language uptake. These are task-specific outcomes, not an all-purpose teacher ranking.
- **Link:** A requested caregiving act → older-sister framing and speculation → later revealed boundary breach

**Basis:** C3P1–35

**Finding:** Sawara's care motive and Susu's carrying request remain particular evidence. The later touching is not licensed by the prior request; Sui's objections and absent explicit assent remain active. 'Since earlier' prevents assigning the act's onset to the intervening manager cut.
- **Link:** Offer of personal care → differing claims of self-sufficiency → bounded invitation

**Basis:** C4P1–29;C5P66–68

**Finding:** Shinju's own competence and corrected household account qualify the sister-care framing; her later occasional permission is a limited future assent rather than continuous access.
- **Link:** Ambiguous sounds and protest → explicit game explanation → noise boundary

**Basis:** C5P1–28

**Finding:** The final room's misunderstanding is textually resolved as game play, rather than converted into a real assault or a manager's confessed fantasy. It supplies no repair of the earlier different conduct.
- **Link:** Round completion → Coney's proposed nighttime activities → Player's refusals

**Basis:** C5P30–42

**Finding:** Managerial restraint occurs in specific decisions; it does not prove that the chaperones know every incident or possess a complete safeguarding structure.
- **Link:** Differentiated recollections and practice plans → all-member growth appraisal → surprise live proposal and rally

**Basis:** C5P43–86

**Finding:** Coney turns her reading of the camp into proposed public work. The mixed response and local agreement support an intention to attempt the live; no performance outcome or universal maturity follows.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Source-explicit dinner, beach recollections, bedtime checks and planned return establish continuity across the front and back camp parts. This does not place them at an exact global date or import later Main outcomes into each chapter's frozen known-then state.
- Beaches, scuba, volleyball, water-pistol play and photographs are retrospectively reported. Peer enthusiasm and Sawara's photo appraisal are attributed, not replaced by unseen media or technical grades. Sui's no-exposure statement remains explicit.
- Musubi's translation explanation is present and praised; its unspecified target language must not become 'English' from inference. School curricula are speakers' local reports. Shizuka's imaginative arithmetic is not evidence of incapacity or a literal belief.
- Susu's request is for carrying. Sawara's interpretation of loneliness is not confirmed by Susu. An act revealed later as already ongoing is not merely an unperformed proposal, and its precise onset is unknown. Sui identifies and objects to it, with no established participation by Sui.
- The managers' black-screen exchange does not state what they hear in the Sawara/Susu room. Their decision to move on does not prove knowledge, consent, endorsement or intervention concerning the final reveal. The reader's later information exceeds what the managers are shown to know.
- Skincare-product suggestions are character advice, not medical recommendations or demonstrated benefit. Shinju's self-sufficiency, household correction and eventual occasional hair-play permission qualify both the carers' and managers' broader sister labels.
- The game explanation resolves the ambiguity in the last room only. It does not convert the source's earlier identified touching into harmless play or cancel the reader's need to retain that unresolved boundary problem.
- Coney's wake-up routine is reported through several responses; no performed weapon, injury or advance agreement is shown. Player's objection and Musubi's report of repeated wake attempts cannot be removed by the amused recollection.
- Seven portrait slots limit the two group reactions. The admitted 777☆SISTERS unit remains twelve-person. Coney's final address neither creates a new lineup nor establishes every absent operational detail.
- Coney and Player's growth appraisal, Hime's continuing practice need, Shizuka's offer and Musubi's wish are distinct. The collective rally is not a performed live, an objective all-member competency grade, an audit of safety, or a READY promotion.

#### Character relations

- **Subject:** Coney and Player

**Finding:** Their chaperone partnership combines playful participation, denied romantic insinuations, selective restraint and a new public-work proposal. Player's claims that a room seems fine or rounds are complete are bounded by what he knows. Coney's enthusiasm for group growth coexists with abrupt planning and his objections.
- **Subject:** Musubi, Shizuka and Hime

**Finding:** An intelligible explanation and negotiated homework plan coexist with continuing dance-learning needs. Hime explicitly wants practice rather than claiming mastery; Shizuka and Musubi offer shared future work. School difference does not become a hierarchy of whole-person competence.
- **Subject:** Sawara, Susu and Sui

**Finding:** Sawara adopts a sister-care role and supplies an unconfirmed loneliness interpretation. Susu requests one kind of help while resisting infantilization. The later intimate act lacks explicit assent, and Sui objects without established participation. This record preserves critical care and consent distinctions rather than generalizing one scene into a timeless personality verdict.
- **Subject:** Sumire, Rona and Shinju

**Finding:** Sumire's helpful self-presentation receives local recognition from Rona; Rona tries offering care with greater confidence. Shinju asserts self-sufficiency, corrects a mistaken household assumption and later permits hair play occasionally. These negotiations do not create guardianship, a household change or unlimited permission.
- **Subject:** Haru, Kajika and Momoka

**Finding:** The final room distributes play and encouragement: Kajika wants another contest, Momoka accepts and Haru supports both. Momoka's fierce game persona does not establish actual aggression toward Kajika, while her next-week camp wish remains a wish.
- **Subject:** 777☆SISTERS and Nanasta

**Finding:** The camp ends with differentiated recollections and a collective rally around a proposed surprise live. The public-facing intention is supported; execution, audience, operational means and universal benefit are still absent. Neither the positive ending nor an existing unit label certifies all private conduct.
